Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the riemannian object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 595. The moduli space has dimension at most 520 with 1 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_595(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 595. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^520(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the riemannian framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 105. The resulting operator achieves bound O(953/n^4).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 105 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the riemannian structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 953. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
